About 60 rows plus the **Network School**. Loaded from a seed list; there is no console screen to add, edit, or remove Schools.\n- **User**: email, argon2 password hash, role (admin or editor), active flag, and a \"must change password\" flag. An Editor has exactly one School; an Admin has none.\n- **Post** and **Page**: School, title, address (slug), Markdown body, Status (draft or published), author, created and updated timestamps. Pages that belong to the Network School also carry a pin order. A \"changed since last Publish\" comparison needs the time of the last Publish to be recorded.\n- Slugs are unique per School, separately for Posts and for Pages.\n\n**Permissions** (enforced in data access, never only in templates)\n- Editor queries are always filtered to their School. A Post or Page of another School returns not-found for every route (view, edit, delete, status change).\n- Editors may create, edit, and delete their School's draft content, and may set published content back to draft. Only the Admin may permanently delete published content.\n- Only the Admin may manage Users, reassign Schools, write Network School content, pin Pages, and run Publish.\n- A User is checked against the database on each request, so deactivation and role changes take effect immediately.\n- No route may rely on the client to name the School; the School comes from the signed-in User, or from the Admin's explicit choice.\n\n**Public site structure** (all paths relative)\n- Home: merged feed of published Posts across Schools, newest first, each labelled with its School.\n- Schools index: all 60 Schools as an alphabetical text list.\n- Per-School page: that School's published Posts and Pages.\n- Individual content at `<school>/<slug>.html`; Posts and Pages share the School's folder, so a School's Post and Page cannot share a slug. This rule is a decision to settle in the data model ticket.\n- Navigation: Pinned Pages first in the Admin's order, then the Schools entry. School Pages appear on the School's own page, not in the top-level nav, so 60 Schools do not clutter it.\n- A reserved slug list protects fixed paths (the home, the Schools index, and the like).\n- Byline shows the School only.\n\n**Publish**\n- Rebuilds the entire output from scratch on every run (existing behaviour of the render function), writing only published rows. Removed or unpublished items disappear.\n- Records the time of the last Publish for the dashboard.\n- Markdown is sanitized before it is rendered anywhere (console preview and public). Script and event-handler content is stripped. External image links are allowed; relative-path rules apply only to the site's own links.\n\n**Console**\n- Dashboards: Editor sees own-School counts (Posts and Pages, draft and published, changed since last Publish). Admin sees Network counts plus a per-School breakdown.\n- Content list filters: Status and type for everyone; School for the Admin.\n- Preview renders the same sanitized HTML as the public site.\n- Forms carry CSRF tokens. Passwords are argon2-hashed; a new User must change a temporary password at first login.\n- Plain-language error messages for slug problems (duplicate suggests the next free address; reserved is refused).\n\n**Sequencing hint for tickets**: each ticket should be a thin vertical slice (model, route, template, test) that is demoable alone, for example login and roles with a School-scoped list, then Post create and edit, then Page and nav, then Publish, then users, then dashboard and filters.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **Good tests assert external behaviour**: HTTP responses, redirects, what appears in the rendered pages, and the files Publish writes. They do not assert on internal functions or SQL.\n- **Seam 1, HTTP**: the existing `client` and `client_as(role)` fixtures. Needs extending with a second School's Editor so cross-School rules are testable, and a seed with several Schools plus the Network School.\n- **Seam 2, Publish**: run Publish against a seeded temporary database into a temporary directory, then read the generated files. Assertions: only published content present; drafts absent; sanitized Markdown; nav built from Pinned Pages; all href and src values relative; unpublished items removed on the next run.\n- Required cross-School tests, per route (view, edit, delete, status change): an Editor of one School gets not-found on another's Post and Page.\n- Prior art: `tests/test_t00.py` (HTTP smoke test) and the module docstring in `tests/conftest.py`.\n- Read each test `/tdd` writes before letting it pass: a test written against a misunderstanding goes green.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Alumni accounts, directory, approval, and per-field privacy (phone, email, occupation). The CMS stores no Alumni data.\n- A US map of Schools. This is a non-functional mockup outside `site/`, with made-up data, only after the CMS works.\n- Image uploads and a media library (external image links are allowed).\n- Comments, search, tags, categories.\n- Scheduled publishing and revision history.\n- Rich-text editing and page builders (excluded by the assignment).\n- Email, password reset by email, and any live deployment of the admin console.\n- Adding, editing, or removing Schools from the console.\n- Multi-site.\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- No new ADRs: nothing beyond ADR-001 and ADR-002 is both hard to reverse and a real trade-off. The Status-versus-Publish split lives here.\n- Known consequences to explain to the client: a published item is live only at the next Publish; unpublishing breaks any old links to it; the public site is open to anyone (no reader login).\n- Editing a published Post changes the stored copy immediately; there is no separate working copy.\n- The seed file of 60 Schools needs real data from the client; use made-up placeholders for demos.\n"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"b5a307cd-72de-4c32-981e-a6e97358eeb2","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011Cg2zp7kLw4VycsyTPaT3i","type":"assistant","uuid":"25170f5a-db99-4b85-a4e4-73cd11efcaaf","timestamp":"2026-10-14T23:29:09.028Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"25170f5a-db99-4b85-a4e4-73cd11efcaaf","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"277199b2-8d02-45e9-939e-5901108bdbbd","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MLvT99K7ucDXaGunnHRE8v","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /tmp/claude-1000/-home-joe-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9/scratchpad/spec.md (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"bb5847aa-8b0d-470d-865e-294e66ad04f5","timestamp":"2026-10-14T23:29:09.070Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"create","filePath":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-joe-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9/scratchpad/spec.md","content":"## Problem Statement\n\nThe Jesuit Schools Network has about 60 member high schools. Their alumni news is scattered across social media accounts and buried on each school's own news page, so alumni connect with classmates but not with the wider network. Each school has one person (usually the head of alumni relations) who writes about their school. The network director, Jennifer LaMaster, supervises but does not write day to day. She needs one place where every School's Posts live together, with a guarantee that one School's Editor can never touch another School's content.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS in two halves, per ADR-001 and ADR-002.\n\n- A **local admin console** where the **Admin** (Jennifer) and **Editors** (one per School) manage **Posts** and **Pages**. Every Post and Page belongs to exactly one **School**; an Editor sees and changes only their own School's content.\n- A **public static site**, produced by **Publish**, showing a merged news feed of all Schools' published Posts, a Schools index, a page per School, and a navigation built from published Pages.\n\nThe CMS holds no records about **Alumni**. Alumni accounts, approval, and per-field privacy are out of scope because the public site is static and the console is never on the internet.\n\nVocabulary is defined in `CONTEXT.md` and used throughout.\n\n## User Stories\n\n**Login and sessions**\n1. As an Admin, I want to sign in with email and password, so that only authorised people can use the console.\n2. As an Editor, I want to sign in and out, so that my School's content is protected on a shared computer.\n3. As a User, I want a wrong password to be rejected with a plain message that does not say which part was wrong, so that accounts cannot be guessed.\n4. As a User with a temporary password, I want to be made to choose my own password at first login, so that the Admin no longer knows it.\n5. As a Deactivated User, I want my existing session to stop working immediately, so that a person who left cannot keep editing.\n6. As an Admin, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a stolen database does not reveal them.\n7. As a User, I want every form to carry a CSRF token, so that another website cannot make my browser change content.\n\n**Roles and Schools**\n8. As an Admin, I want the 60 Schools loaded from a seed list with city and state, so that I do not have to type them in.\n9. As an Admin, I want a Network School to exist alongside the 60, so that network-wide Pages like \"About\" and \"How to join\" have an owner.\n10. As an Editor, I want to be assigned to exactly one School, so that my permissions match my job.\n11. As an Editor, I want to see only my School's Posts and Pages, drafts included, so that I am not distracted by other Schools.\n12. As an Editor, I want a request for another School's Post or Page to look like it does not exist, so that I cannot probe what other Schools have.\n13. As an Admin, I want to act on every School's content, so that I can supervise and fix mistakes.\n14. As an Admin, I want only me to be able to write to the Network School, so that network-wide Pages are not changed by an Editor.\n\n**User management (Admin only)**\n15. As an Admin, I want to create Users and set a temporary password, so that Editors can start without email.\n16. As an Admin, I want to choose a School for each new Editor, so that their scope is correct from the first login.\n17. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role and reassign an Editor's School, so that staff changes are handled.\n18. As an Admin, I want content to stay with its School when an Editor is reassigned, so that nothing silently moves.\n19. As an Admin, I want to deactivate a User without deleting what they wrote, so that a School's history survives staff turnover.\n20. As an Editor, I want user-management screens to be unavailable to me, so that I cannot create accounts or change roles.\n\n**Posts**\n21. As an Editor, I want to create a Post with title, Markdown body, and Status, so that I can write news about my School.\n22. As an Editor, I want the address of a Post generated from its title, so that I never have to think about slugs.\n23. As an Editor, I want a duplicate address to be fixed for me by suggesting the next free one, so that I am not blocked by a rule I do not understand.\n24. As an Editor, I want a reserved address to be refused with a plain message, so that I cannot break the site's fixed pages.\n25. As an Editor, I want the Post to record who wrote it and when it was created and last changed, so that there is accountability.\n26. As an Editor, I want to edit a published Post in place, so that I can fix a typo without taking it offline.\n27. As an Editor, I want to delete my School's draft Posts, so that I can clean up.\n28. As an Editor, I want to set a published Post back to draft instead of deleting it, so that I can withdraw it safely.\n29. As an Admin, I want to permanently delete published content, so that removal of live content needs a second person.\n30. As an Alumnus, I want to see a Post labelled by School and not by author name, so that individual staff are not exposed.\n\n**Pages**\n31. As an Editor, I want to create and manage Pages for my School with the same rules as Posts, so that standing information is available.\n32. As an Admin, I want to pin Network School Pages at the front of the navigation, so that \"About\" and \"How to join\" come first.\n33. As an Alumnus, I want School Pages listed alphabetically after the pinned ones, so that I can find things predictably.\n\n**Admin console**\n34. As an Editor, I want a dashboard with counts of my School's draft and published Posts and Pages, so that I see my School's state at a glance.\n35. As an Admin, I want a dashboard for the whole Network with a per-School breakdown, so that I can see which Schools are not posting.\n36. As an Admin, I want a count of items changed since the last Publish, so that I know whether Publish is needed.\n37. As an Editor, I want a content list I can filter by Status and by type, so that I can find what to work on.\n38. As an Admin, I want to filter the content list by School as well, so that I can look at one School.\n39. As an Editor, I want a preview of my Markdown exactly as the public site will render it, sanitized, so that I know what alumni will see.\n\n**Publish and the public site**\n40. As an Admin, I want to run Publish to export all Schools' published content at once, so that I control what goes live.\n41. As an Editor, I want setting a Post's Status to published to mean \"ready\", not \"live\", so that I understand it appears at the next Publish.\n42. As an Alumnus, I want a home page with a merged feed of every School's published Posts, newest first, so that I see the whole network in one place.\n43. As an Alumnus, I want a Schools index listing all 60 Schools as plain text, so that I can find mine without using a map.\n44. As an Alumnus, I want a page per School listing its published Posts and Pages, so that I can follow my own School.\n45. As an Alumnus, I want the site to contain only published content, so that unfinished work is never visible.\n46. As an Alumnus, I want unpublished or deleted items to disappear at the next Publish, so that withdrawn content stays withdrawn.\n47. As an Alumnus, I want Markdown rendered safely, with scripts stripped, so that the site cannot attack me.\n48. As an Alumnus, I want images linked from other sites to display in Posts, so that school news can have photos.\n49. As an Alumnus, I want every link on the site to work when it is served from a subfolder, so that nothing is broken on the hosting platform.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n**Domain model** (terms as in `CONTEXT.md`)\n- **School**: name, short name, city, state, and a stable URL name. About 60 rows plus the **Network School**. Loaded from a seed list; there is no console screen to add, edit, or remove Schools.\n- **User**: email, argon2 password hash, role (admin or editor), active flag, and a \"must change password\" flag. An Editor has exactly one School; an Admin has none.\n- **Post** and **Page**: School, title, address (slug), Markdown body, Status (draft or published), author, created and updated timestamps. Pages that belong to the Network School also carry a pin order. A \"changed since last Publish\" comparison needs the time of the last Publish to be recorded.\n- Slugs are unique per School, separately for Posts and for Pages.\n\n**Permissions** (enforced in data access, never only in templates)\n- Editor queries are always filtered to their School. A Post or Page of another School returns not-found for every route (view, edit, delete, status change).\n- Editors may create, edit, and delete their School's draft content, and may set published content back to draft. This rule is a decision to settle in the data model ticket.\n- Navigation: Pinned Pages first in the Admin's order, then the Schools entry. School Pages appear on the School's own page, not in the top-level nav, so 60 Schools do not clutter it.\n- A reserved slug list protects fixed paths (the home, the Schools index, and the like).\n- Byline shows the School only.\n\n**Publish**\n- Rebuilds the entire output from scratch on every run (existing behaviour of the render function), writing only published rows. Removed or unpublished items disappear.\n- Records the time of the last Publish for the dashboard.\n- Markdown is sanitized before it is rendered anywhere (console preview and public). Script and event-handler content is stripped. External image links are allowed; relative-path rules apply only to the site's own links.\n\n**Console**\n- Dashboards: Editor sees own-School counts (Posts and Pages, draft and published, changed since last Publish). Admin sees Network counts plus a per-School breakdown.\n- Content list filters: Status and type for everyone; School for the Admin.\n- Preview renders the same sanitized HTML as the public site.\n- Forms carry CSRF tokens. Passwords are argon2-hashed; a new User must change a temporary password at first login.\n- Plain-language error messages for slug problems (duplicate suggests the next free address; reserved is refused).\n\n**Sequencing hint for tickets**: each ticket should be a thin vertical slice (model, route, template, test) that is demoable alone, for example login and roles with a School-scoped list, then Post create and edit, then Page and nav, then Publish, then users, then dashboard and filters.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **Good tests assert external behaviour**: HTTP responses, redirects, what appears in the rendered pages, and the files Publish writes. {"parentUuid":"541fdecd-f939-401c-90b0-718ca6fd8f40","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg2zs8LuBP5EJo4k92Q8A","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The spec is published as **issue #1**: https://github.com/joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/1. They didn't exist on the repo.\n\nIt has 49 user stories, the implementation decisions, the two test seams, and the out-of-scope list.\n\n**One thing I settled myself.** URLs were `<school>/<slug>.html`, but your Q6 answer made slugs unique per School for Posts and for Pages separately. That would let a Post and a Page collide on one file, so I used `<school>/posts/<slug>.html` and `<school>/pages/<slug>.html`. Please check that against what you intended.\n\n**Next steps:**\n1. Read the issue on GitHub. If it says something you didn't decide, tell me and I'll fix it now, since the tickets inherit it.\n2. Run `/to-tickets` in this same window. {"parentUuid":"45a4005a-c9f0-47cc-aae2-65b8bd0d76e9","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"0d536526-84b5-4cdc-bb1e-237983b553c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/to-tickets\n\n# To Tickets\n\nBreak a plan, spec, or conversation into a set of **tickets**: tracer-bullet vertical slices, each declaring the tickets that **block** it.\n\nThe issue tracker and triage label vocabulary should have been provided to you. If not, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Gather context\n\nWork from whatever is already in the conversation context. If the user passes a reference (a spec path, an issue number or URL) as an argument, fetch it and read its full body and comments.\n\n### 2. Explore the codebase (optional)\n\nIf you have not already explored the codebase, do so to understand the current state of the code. Ticket titles and descriptions should use the project's domain glossary vocabulary, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\nLook for opportunities to prefactor the code to make the implementation easier. \"Make the change easy, then make the easy change.\"\n\n### 3. Draft vertical slices\n\nBreak the work into **tracer bullet** tickets.\n\n<vertical-slice-rules>\n\n- Each slice cuts a narrow but COMPLETE path through every layer (schema, API, UI, tests): vertical, NOT a horizontal slice of one layer\n- A completed slice is demoable or verifiable on its own\n- Each slice is sized to fit in a single fresh context window\n- Any prefactoring should be done first\n\n</vertical-slice-rules>\n\nGive each ticket its **blocking edges**: the other tickets that must complete before it can start. A ticket with no blockers can start immediately.\n\n**Wide refactors are the exception to vertical slicing.** A **wide refactor** is one mechanical change (rename a column, retype a shared symbol) whose **blast radius** fans across the whole codebase, so a single edit breaks thousands of call sites at once and no vertical slice can land green. Don't force it into a tracer bullet; sequence it as **expand–contract**. First expand: add the new form beside the old so nothing breaks. Then migrate the call sites over in batches sized by blast radius (per package, per directory), each batch its own ticket blocked by the expand, keeping CI green batch to batch because the old form still exists. Finally contract: delete the old form once no caller remains, in a ticket blocked by every migrate batch. When even the batches can't stay green alone, keep the sequence but let them share an integration branch that all block a final integrate-and-verify ticket; green is promised only there.\n\n### 4. Quiz the user\n\nPresent the proposed breakdown as a numbered list. For each ticket, show:\n\n- **Title**: short descriptive name\n- **Blocked by**: which other tickets (if any) must complete first\n- **What it delivers**: the end-to-end behaviour this ticket makes work\n\nAsk the user:\n\n- Does the granularity feel right? (too coarse / too fine)\n- Are the blocking edges correct: does each ticket only depend on tickets that genuinely gate it?\n- Should any tickets be merged or split further?\n\nIterate until the user approves the breakdown.\n\n### 5. Publish the tickets to the configured tracker\n\nPublish the approved tickets. **How** depends on the tracker `/setup-matt-pocock-skills` configured; the tickets are the same either way, only the shape of the blocking edges changes:\n\n- **Local files** → write one file per ticket under `.scratch/<feature-slug>/issues/<NN>-<slug>.md`, numbered from `01` in dependency order (blockers first). Each file's \"Blocked by\" lists the numbers/titles it depends on. Use the per-ticket file template below: one ticket per file, never a single combined file.\n- **A real issue tracker (GitHub, Linear, …)** → publish one issue per ticket in dependency order (blockers first) so each ticket's blocking edges can reference real identifiers. Use the platform's native blocking / sub-issue relationship where it has one; otherwise set each ticket's \"Blocked by\" to the blocking issues. Apply the `ready-for-agent` triage label unless instructed otherwise; the tickets are agent-grabbable by construction.\n\nWork the **frontier**: any ticket whose blockers are all done. For a purely linear chain that means top to bottom.\n\nDo NOT close or modify any parent issue.\n\n<local-ticket-template>\n\n# <NN>: <Ticket title>\n\n**What to build:** the end-to-end behaviour this ticket makes work, from the user's perspective, not a layer-by-layer implementation list.\n\n**Blocked by:** the numbers/titles of the tickets that gate this one, or \"None (can start immediately)\".\n\n**Status:** ready-for-agent\n\n- [ ] Acceptance criterion 1\n- [ ] Acceptance criterion 2\n\n</local-ticket-template>\n\n<issue-template>\n\n## Parent\n\nA reference to the parent issue on the tracker (if the source was an existing issue, otherwise omit this section).\n\n## What to build\n\nThe end-to-end behaviour this ticket makes work, from the user's perspective, not layer-by-layer implementation.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Criterion 1\n- [ ] Criterion 2\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- A reference to each blocking ticket, or \"None (can start immediately)\".\n\n</issue-template>\n\nIn either form, avoid specific file paths or code snippets: they go stale fast. Exception: if a prototype produced a snippet that encodes a decision more precisely than prose can (state machine, reducer, schema, type shape), inline it and note briefly that it came from a prototype. Trim to the decision-rich parts, not a working demo, just the important bits.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"3d29a7ed-4865-4cb8-b2a5-cb7ac6533f28","timestamp":"2026-10-14T23:30:13.060Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"ad917772-377a-496d-a8fe-7dbb3b14a6ab","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_011a8yQE2SsTjyorRC4TKxj9","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"a0b7b699-cdda-4719-8542-1ca40a22fed6","timestamp":"2026-10-14T23:30:31.578Z","session_id":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"a0b7b699-cdda-4719-8542-1ca40a22fed6","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg2zwxg7WBY56JPNWQAu5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've set the phase to `tickets` and read spec #1. Here is the proposed breakdown: 8 tickets, each a thin path through model, route, template and tests. T00 (the hello-admin skeleton) is already done. The spec is #1, so GitHub will number these #2 to #9, but I'm calling them T01 to T08 as your commit convention does.\n\n**T01: Sign in, and the Schools exist**\n- **Blocked by:** none\n- **Delivers:** the Admin and an Editor can sign in and out, and a new User must change a temporary password at first login. Passwords are argon2-hashed and forms carry CSRF tokens. The seed loads a few Schools plus the Network School, with a second School's Editor for the cross-School tests. The console home shows who you are and which School you belong to.\n\n**T02: Editors write Posts for their own School**\n- **Blocked by:** T01\n- **Delivers:** an Editor can create, edit and list their School's Posts with a draft or published Status. The address is generated from the title, a duplicate gets the next free one, and a reserved one is refused. The preview renders sanitized Markdown. Another School's Post returns not-found on view and edit, and this is tested with the two Editors.\n\n**T03: Publish puts Posts on the public site**\n- **Blocked by:** T02\n- **Delivers:** the Admin runs Publish. It rebuilds the site from scratch and writes the merged home feed, the Schools index, a page per School and each Post, with only published Posts and relative paths only. The time of the last Publish is recorded. T01 to T03 are the Stage 1 MVP.\n\n**T04: Withdraw and delete rules**\n- **Blocked by:** T02\n- **Delivers:** an Editor can delete their School's drafts and set published Posts back to draft. Only the Admin can permanently delete published content. An unpublished item disappears at the next Publish. The Admin gets a School filter on the content list, and cross-School delete attempts return not-found.\n\n**T05: Pages and navigation**\n- **Blocked by:** T03\n- **Delivers:** Pages follow the same rules as Posts. The Network School owns the network-wide Pages, which only the Admin can write and pin. Published Pages show in the nav, with Pinned Pages first and School Pages alphabetical on each School's page.\n\n**T06: User management**\n- **Blocked by:** T01\n- **Delivers:** the Admin creates Users, sets the temporary password, chooses an Editor's School, changes roles and reassigns Schools. Deactivation takes effect immediately, and content stays with its School. Editors get not-found or redirected away from the screens.\n\n**T07: Dashboards and filters**\n- **Blocked by:** T04, T05\n- **Delivers:** an Editor sees counts for their own School and the Admin sees Network counts with a per-School breakdown. Both see \"changed since last Publish\". The content list filters by Status and type.\n\n**T08: Demo data and the final safety net**\n- **Blocked by:** T05, T06\n- **Delivers:** the seed holds 60 placeholder Schools. End-to-end tests prove that drafts never reach `site/`, every href and src is relative across all content, Markdown with scripts is stripped everywhere it renders, and every state-changing form has a CSRF token.\n\nT04 and T06 can run in either order after their blockers.\n\n**Questions for you:**\n1. Is the granularity right? T01 is the biggest, because login, seed and CSRF travel together, and I could split it into \"login\" and \"seed + Schools\".\n2. Are the blocking edges right? For example, T07 waits on both T04 and T05 because its counts cover Posts and Pages.\n3. Should anything be merged or split? T04 could fold into T02 if you'd rather have fewer tickets, though T02 would then be larger.\n\nOnce you approve, I'll publish them in dependency order with the `ticket` and `ready-for-agent` labels and native blocking edges where GitHub supports them."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":942,"cache_read_input_tokens":83481,"output_tokens":1312,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":942,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"global","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1312,"cache_read_input_tokens":83481,"cache_creation_input_tokens":942,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":942},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"f0b813a5-5eee-4736-9c74-e3c939be16da","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011Cg2zwxQzLWy491n67tMqV","attributionSkill":"to-tickets","type":"assistant","uuid":"125ab9d4-302a-40cb-ad17-7814b05cdac4","timestamp":"2026-10-14T23:30:31.577Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"db29633b-29d7-4754-8499-466e9da610c6","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg2zxzscAtCYmcGmwWy7G","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01FBvp4b6PiUsBqTbZvQexfB","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"mkdir -p /tmp/claude-1000/-home-joe-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9/scratchpad && cd /home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && cat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-joe-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9/scratchpad/mk.py <<'E'\nimport subprocess, json\nT = [\n(\"T01\",\"Sign in, and the Schools exist\",[],\n\"The Admin and an Editor can sign in and out. A User with a temporary password must choose their own at first login. The seed loads a few Schools, the Network School, an Admin, and Editors for two different Schools (needed for later cross-School tests). The console home shows who is signed in and which School they belong to.\",\n[\"Sign in with correct credentials succeeds; wrong credentials are rejected with a message that does not say which part was wrong\",\"Passwords are stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged\",\"A User flagged to change password is redirected to a change-password form until they set a new one\",\"Sign out ends the session\",\"Every state-changing form carries a CSRF token and a request without it is rejected\",\"Console routes redirect an unauthenticated visitor to sign in\",\"The seed provides Schools, the Network School, an Admin, and Editors for two Schools; `client_as` can sign in as each\",\"Console home shows the User's role and School\"]),\n(\"T02\",\"Editors write Posts for their own School\",[\"T01\"],\n\"An Editor can create, edit, and list their own School's Posts, with a draft or published Status and a preview of the sanitized Markdown. Another School's Posts do not exist as far as the Editor can tell.\",\n[\"Create a Post with title, Markdown body, and Status; author and created/updated timestamps are recorded\",\"The address is generated from the title; a duplicate within the School gets the next free address (e.g. -2); a reserved address is refused with a plain-language message\",\"The same slug may exist in two different Schools\",\"An Editor's list shows only their School's Posts, drafts included\",\"An Editor requesting another School's Post to view or edit gets not-found (tested with the two Editors)\",\"Editing a published Post changes it in place\",\"Preview renders the Markdown as sanitized HTML; script and event-handler content is stripped\",\"An Admin can create and edit Posts for any School, choosing the School explicitly\"]),\n(\"T03\",\"Publish puts Posts on the public site\",[\"T02\"],\n\"The Admin runs Publish. The public site shows a merged home feed of all Schools' published Posts (newest first, labelled with School), a Schools index, a page per School, and each published Post. Part of the Stage 1 MVP.\",\n[\"Publish rebuilds the output from scratch; only published Posts appear and drafts never do\",\"Home lists published Posts from all Schools newest first, labelled by School and not by author\",\"Schools index lists every School as plain text; each School has a page listing its published Posts\",\"Individual Posts are written under their School's posts folder\",\"Every href and src the site owns is relative; none starts with `/`\",\"Markdown is sanitized in the output; external image links are kept\",\"The time of the last Publish is recorded\",\"Only the Admin can run Publish; an Editor cannot\",\"Tested at the Publish seam against a temporary output directory\"]),\n(\"T04\",\"Withdraw and delete rules\",[\"T02\"],\n\"Editors can delete their School's draft Posts and set published Posts back to draft. Only the Admin can permanently delete published content. The Admin can filter the content list by School.\",\n[\"An Editor can delete a draft Post of their School\",\"An Editor cannot permanently delete a published Post; they can set it back to draft\",\"An Admin can permanently delete a published Post\",\"An Editor attempting to delete, unpublish, or change the Status of another School's Post gets not-found\",\"After a Publish, a Post set back to draft or deleted no longer appears in the output\",\"The Admin's content list can be filtered by School; an Editor has no School filter\"]),\n(\"T05\",\"Pages and navigation\",[\"T03\"],\n\"Pages follow the same rules as Posts. The Network School owns the network-wide Pages, which only the Admin can write and pin. Published Pages appear in the public navigation.\",\n[\"Editors create, edit, list, and delete (draft only) their School's Pages with the same address and scoping rules as Posts\",\"An Editor cannot view or write another School's Page, nor any Network School Page\",\"The Admin can write Network School Pages and pin them in a chosen order\",\"Publish writes Pages under their School's pages folder\",\"The navigation shows Pinned Pages first in the Admin's order, then a Schools entry; a School's own Pages are listed alphabetically on its School page\",\"Unpublished Pages are absent from output and nav after the next Publish\",\"All paths remain relative\"]),\n(\"T06\",\"User management\",[\"T01\"],\n\"The Admin creates Users, sets a temporary password, assigns an Editor's School, changes roles, reassigns Schools, and deactivates Users. Editors cannot reach these screens.\",\n[\"Admin can create a User with role, temporary password, and (for Editors) a School; the User must change the password at first login\",\"Admin can change a User's role and reassign an Editor's School; existing content stays with its School\",\"Admin can deactivate a User; a Deactivated User cannot sign in and an already-signed-in session stops working on its next request\",\"Deactivating a User does not delete their Posts or Pages\",\"An Editor requesting any user-management route is redirected or refused\",\"Passwords are never shown or logged\"]),\n(\"T07\",\"Dashboards and filters\",[\"T04\",\"T05\"],\n\"Editors see counts for their own School; the Admin sees Network counts with a per-School breakdown. Both see how much has changed since the last Publish. The content list filters by Status and type.\",\n[\"Editor dashboard shows draft and published counts of Posts and Pages for their School only\",\"Admin dashboard shows Network totals and a per-School breakdown\",\"Both show the count of items changed since the last Publish\",\"The content list filters by Status and by type (Post or Page) for everyone\",\"Counts and lists never include another School's content for an Editor\"]),\n(\"T08\",\"Demo data and the final safety net\",[\"T05\",\"T06\"],\n\"Demo data covers all 60 placeholder Schools, and end-to-end tests prove the hard constraints across the whole site.\",\n[\"Seed script loads 60 made-up Schools with city and state, plus the Network School\",\"End-to-end test: with drafts of every kind in the database, none appears anywhere in the output\",\"End-to-end test: every href and src in the output is relative\",\"End-to-end test: Markdown containing scripts or event handlers is stripped in preview and in the output\",\"Every state-changing form in the console carries a CSRF token (checked across all forms)\",\"The README explains that content goes live only at the next Publish\"]),\n]\nnum={}\ndef sh(*a): return subprocess.run(a,capture_output=True,text=True)\nfor tid,title,blk,what,ac in T:\n    b=\"## Parent\\n\\nSpec #1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\n\"+what+\"\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n\"+\"\\n\".join(\"- [ ] \"+c for c in ac)+\"\\n\\n## Blocked by\\n\\n\"\n    b+= \"\\n\".join(f\"- #{num[x]} ({x})\" for x in blk) if blk else \"None (can start immediately)\"\n    r=sh(\"gh\",\"issue\",\"create\",\"--title\",f\"{tid}: {title}\",\"--label\",\"ticket\",\"--label\",\"ready-for-agent\",\"--body\",b)\n    n=int(r.stdout.strip().split('/')[-1]); num[tid]=n; print(tid,n,r.stderr.strip())\n    for x in blk:\n        bid=sh(\"gh\",\"api\",f\"repos/joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/{num[x]}\",\"--jq\",\".id\").stdout.strip()\n        e=sh(\"gh\",\"api\",\"--method\",\"POST\",f\"repos/joehaugh/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/{n}/dependencies/blocked_by\",\"-F\",f\"issue_id={bid}\")\n        print(\"  edge\",x,\"->\",tid,\"ok\" if e.returncode==0 else \"FAIL \"+e.stderr.strip()[:100])\nE\npython3 /tmp/claude-1000/-home-joe-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9/scratchpad/mk.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":36,"cache_read_input_tokens":85735,"output_tokens":3035,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":197},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":36,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"global","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3035,"cache_read_input_tokens":85735,"cache_creation_input_tokens":36,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":36},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01FBvp4b6PiUsBqTbZvQexfB":{"command":"mkdir -p /tmp/claude-1000/-home-joe-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9/scratchpad && cd /home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && cat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-joe-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9/scratchpad/mk.py <<'E'\nimport subprocess, json\nT = [\n(\"T01\",\"Sign in, and the Schools exist\",[],\n\"The Admin and an Editor can sign in and out. {"parentUuid":"269d3ccf-b776-4cad-9c62-f47a556e960e","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg312PJK98jEv114qRojf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"All 8 tickets are published, #2 to #9, each labelled `ticket` and `ready-for-agent`. Each ticket's body links to spec #1 and has acceptance criteria. I also added GitHub's native \"blocked by\" edges, and every edge was accepted.\n\n| Issue | Ticket | Blocked by |\n|---|---|---|\n| #2 | T01 Sign in, and the Schools exist | none |\n| #3 | T02 Editors write Posts for their own School | #2 |\n| #4 | T03 Publish puts Posts on the public site | #3 |\n| #5 | T04 Withdraw and delete rules | #3 |\n| #6 | T05 Pages and navigation | #4 |\n| #7 | T06 User management | #2 |\n| #8 | T07 Dashboards and filters | #5, #6 |\n| #9 | T08 Demo data and the final safety net | #6, #7 |\n\n`gh issue list --label ticket` showed only 7 on my first check. #9 does exist and is open with both labels, so I think the default list just cut it off. Run `gh issue list --label ticket --limit 20` to confirm it shows for you.\n\n**To do now:** read the tickets on GitHub. Anything you didn't decide is easiest to fix before work starts.\n\n**Then, for each ticket,** use a fresh session:\n```\n/clear\n/implement #2\n```\nStart with #2 (T01), since it blocks #3 and #7. `CLAUDE.md` wants the phase file set to the ticket id when a stage starts, so I'll write `T01` into `.claude/state/phase` when you begin it. Before closing a ticket, post the `/code-review` findings on that issue, and make the commit message start `T01:` and end `Closes #2`.\n\nIf you want T01 to count as part of Stage 1, T01 to T03 are the MVP set that gets tagged `mp2-mvp`."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":214,"cache_read_input_tokens":89255,"output_tokens":626,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":214,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"global","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":626,"cache_read_input_tokens":89255,"cache_creation_input_tokens":214,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":214},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"ac48cb36-af3e-4c13-a3ec-1de49e3ec9fd","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011Cg312P2hD4j7M2w6PUqtk","type":"assistant","uuid":"207c9bc6-71e0-439b-ab33-3eccaf22232c","timestamp":"2026-10-14T23:31:26.784Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"86c96d58-6503-4970-83eb-16ca927e0436","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/joe/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"d0a23431-29b1-4338-8f58-72449daa24f9","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
